record, not the completed graph proposed in `plan.md`.

| Carrier_Indexes | Main | The index of a carrier by a key, the notion of DECISIONS.md "A refinement applies a notion; an index is one", checked. Locale `carrier_index`: a carrier's content is a relation `holds c q v` from queries to values, claimed under a formation parameter `formed`; a carrier discharges obligation (1) `distinguishes` (the key injective on the queries `Q`; `distinguishes_by_left_inverse` gives it from `unkey (key x)=x`) and obligation (2) `represents` (the member equation at every key: `search (build c) k v ⟷ (∃q∈Q. key q=k ∧ holds c q v)`), and then obtains the laws as theorems: `query_search` and `query_fibre` (one built index answers every query with the carrier's fibre), `unkeyed_search`, `content_determines_search` (equal content, equal search at every key: row and insertion order are not subjects); law 1 is the signature, only the key's type is searched by. `carrier_index_through_key`: an index of the key image searched by the keys is an index of the carrier through a key that distinguishes. Extension `updated_carrier_index` (obligation 4): `update_at`, `update_preserves_else`, `update_represents`; replacement and insertion laws are its instances through the `change` parameter. Extension `native_carrier_index`: a search site exact at every key term and every index (`site_exact`, with `inj present`), `site_query`, `site_refuses_unpresented`; `index_search_sound` is the one-directional form. Obligation (3), one equation per operation a use needs, is a use's own proof from these facts. Corollaries the uses' forms instantiate: `queries_search` (every query of a set found iff each holds some value), `lookup_found` and `lookup_queries_found` (where the search is an optional lookup, a premise `search i k v ⟷ look i k=Some v`: the lookup is `≠ None` iff the query holds some value), `found_pairs` (the found pairs are the keyed image of the content: a carrier's whole-set contract in one step). No simp, intro or code attributes. |
| Established_Premises | HOL-Library.FSet | A check made where its premise is established, the first notion of DECISIONS.md "The in-place refinements apply two notions", checked. Locale `established_premise original premise body`: obligation (1) `exact` (`premise x ⟹ original x = body x`), which a use proves; at an enclosing operation or a constructor's contract a use gets exactness at the established value with its establishing fact. Extension `checked_premise … refusal`: obligation (2) `refused` (`¬premise x ⟹ original x = refusal x`), giving `checked_at_entry` (the code equation `original x = (if premise x then body x else refusal x)`) and `checked_through` (the check hoisted through any consumer `t`). `checked_union`: a union over a family refused by `{||}` outside `P` is `(if P then ffUnion (fimage T Y) else {||})`. A premise on a later argument is instantiated with the constant applied to the arguments before it; the code equation is stated at the premise's arity. No simp, intro or code attributes; nothing about cost. |
| Candidate_Generators | HOL-Library.FSet | A generator of the accepted candidates, the second notion of DECISIONS.md "The in-place refinements apply two notions", checked. Locale `candidate_generator space accepts generated`: obligations `sound` (a generated accepted candidate lies in the space) and `complete` (every accepted candidate of the space is generated), giving `accepted_generated` (`ffilter accepts generated = ffilter accepts space`). Extension `tight_candidate_generator`: obligation `tight` (every generated candidate is accepted), giving `generated_accepted` (`generated = ffilter accepts space`). `singleton_tight_generator` (the guarded candidate is the tight generator of a one-candidate space) and `accepted_singleton` (`ffilter accepts {|x|} = (if accepts x then {|x|} else {||})`); the empty space's law is `RRA_Selection.ffilter_empty_set`. No simp, intro or code attributes; nothing about cost. |
| Finite_Functional_Enumeration | RRA_Finite_Artifacts, Finite_Singleton_Selection | Ordered keys enumerate every row of a finite functional relation without imposing an order on its values; the executable selector accepts repeated encodings of the same value. |
| Functional_Enumeration_Indexes | Carrier_Indexes, Finite_Functional_Enumeration | The index notion interpreted in a finite functional relation, formed by `finite_relation_functional`, keyed by the identity: `functional_rows_index` (index `finite_functional_rows`, search `(k,v)∈set rows`, from `finite_functional_rows_exact`) and `functional_option_index` (index `finite_relation_option`, search `f k=Some v`, from `finite_relation_option_correct`); law 1 as an instance, only the keys ordered. A use cites `functional_rows_index.query_search` and the other laws of `carrier_index`. `Finite_Ordered_Representatives` is not interpreted: its map selects the least key of a value's fibre, not the relation's content. |

| Factor_Finite_Data_Syntax | Factor_Finite_Artifact_Enumeration, Factor_Executable_Data_Values, Factor_Complete_Data_Quotation, RRA_Finite_Syntax_Construction, Natural_Binary_Digits | The executable complete data quotation. The nodes of the data syntax are numbered by a counter in construction order and addressed by the binary digits of their number (`compact_syntax_address`, injective, formed, the root keeps the empty address); the numbering is exactly an interval over the carrier (`data_syntax_carrier`, `data_syntax_address_injective`), and the finite constructors decode to the data syntax readdressed by it (`finite_data_syntax_sound`, `finite_data_syntax_exact`), so every returned artifact is a complete data quotation at the empty root (`finite_data_syntax_complete_quotation`). Target-bearing terms are rejected; malformed payload bytes are kept for the separate formation and admission checks. Before, children were addressed by prefixing, so an address grew with its depth and a quotation with the square of a data list. Reuse (The executable quotation is compact — 2026-09-19): The executable complete data quotation is the data syntax readdressed. `data_syntax_position` numbers the nodes by a counter in construction order and `data_syntax_carrier` proves that the numbering is exactly an interval over the carrier, so it is injective; `compact_syntax_address` writes a number in binary digits (reusing `natural_binary_digits`; injective, formed, the root keeps the empty address). `finite_data_syntax_sound`/`_exact` state the result as `push_object` of the syntax by that map, and `finite_data_syntax_complete_quotation` keeps its statement, so every consumer of the quotation contract is unchanged. The interval argument applies to any syntax whose construction addresses children by prefixing: the native compiler's pattern, schema and definition syntax is the next instance. |

| Factor_Demanded_Graph_Readings | Finite_Demanded_Closures, Factor_Recovered_Graph_Sharing | The second instance of the demanded reading of `Finite_Demanded_Closures`: a recovered proof graph reads a proof node only at the sites its root reaches through the premises the nodes discharge (`finite_proof_site_reading`, `finite_proof_children`). The node grammar supplies the reading's boundary (`finite_proof_reading_position`: a node is read from a record at its use, so its site is a position of the environment); the universe formulation's sites are the traversal's (`finite_native_proof_sites_rooted`, through the converse of the discharge edges) and so are its rows (`finite_demanded_proof_readings`); the traversal is the code of `finite_recovered_graph`, with environment formation checked once. Before, a node was read at every position of the environment, including every address of every literal target, so replaying a judgment on a whole artifact cost its square. Reuse (The package reading is demanded — 2026-09-19): The second instance: a recovered proof graph reads a node only at the sites its root reaches through the premises the nodes discharge (successors: the children a node's premises cite). Found by attribution: replaying a judgment on a whole artifact read a node at every address of the literal (at 401, 801 and 1,601 addresses 0.35, 2.5 and 19.6 seconds; the demanded graph reading takes 0.011, 0.024 and 0.050). |
| Factor_Finite_Accumulated_Data_Syntax | Factor_Finite_Data_Syntax, Factor_Finite_Syntax_Accumulation | The executable code of the complete data quotation: each row is constructed at its final compact address, threading the node counter, and accumulated once (`finite_data_syntax_rows_exact`, `finite_data_syntax_accumulated`); each row family is then listed once in its canonical order (`finite_data_syntax_accumulated_code`), so later listings, comparisons and formation checks recognize the listing in one pass instead of sorting it again. Every optional result is unchanged. Reuse (The executable quotation is compact — 2026-09-19): The prefix construction had emitted its rows in ascending order by accident of its addresses; compact rows arrive in construction order, and without the listing every later listing, comparison and formation check of a quotation sorted it again (at 4,000 rows, twenty listings 0.446 against 0.027 seconds, twenty equalities 0.849 against 0.061). Canonical order is a property the consumers' fast paths read (`Finite_Sorted_Set_Execution`), so a producer of finite sets states it where it constructs them. |
| Factor_Finite_Syntax_Accumulation | Factor_Finite_Artifact_Enumeration, RRA_Finite_Syntax_Construction, "HOL-Library.List_Lexorder", "HOL-Library.Product_Lexorder" | Complete syntax rows accumulated once: payload rows and pair rows stated by the node's root, two ports and the roots of its two children, so every addressing of the syntax accumulates through the same rows; their objects are the original syntax join and attachment. Rows listed again in their canonical order present the same object (`finite_syntax_rows_object_listed`). Reuse (The executable quotation is compact — 2026-09-19): Pair rows stated by a node's root, two ports and two child roots (`finite_syntax_rows_pair`), so accumulated rows serve every addressing of the syntax; the prefix-specific pair accumulation was removed as unused. |

## Native source development

| Theory | Imports | Responsibility |
|---|---|---|
| Factor_Development_Criterion_Sources | Factor_Development_Cases, Factor_Finite_Ground_Source, Factor_Finite_System_Unions | Reusable construction of native criteria with exact original source meaning: the ground condition of a finite family is a program condition (`finite_ground_condition_program`); a program of its own is installed together with the guard source (`finite_standalone_condition`, `_total`, `_exact`: its entry keeps its own meaning). |
| Native_Collection_Programs | Factor_Development_Criterion_Sources | Native definitions over native coordinates: ordinary rules whose conclusion binds every variable their premises use (`finite_native_rule`), a family of them at one site with its local contract proved once (`native_rule_family`: `native_step`, `holds_cases`), the collection notions membership (`native_member_program.exact`), every element in a context (the shared `context_list_rule_relation`, presented by `native_every_program.relation_equation`), some element (`native_some_program.exact`) and the value a keyed table holds (`native_keyed_search_program.exact`), and a program as the families at its sites (`finite_rule_program`). Reuse (The selection question evaluates native readiness — 2026-09-19): Native definitions over native coordinates: ordinary rules whose conclusion binds every variable their premises use (`finite_native_rule`), a family of rules at one site with its unfolding and step proved once (`native_rule_family`: `holds_cases`, `step`, `native_step`), the collection notions membership (`native_member_program.exact`), every element in a context (`native_every_program`, presenting the shared `context_list_rule_relation`), some element (`native_some_program.exact`) and the value a keyed table holds (`native_keyed_search_program.exact`), each with an `unfold` for rule applications supported in any relation, which least-fixed-point arguments over programs composed of them consume; a program is the families at its sites (`finite_rule_program`), and a formed one with distinct sites is a `native_rule_family` at each (`finite_rule_program_family`), which every rule program instantiates. |
| Native_Path_Stores | Native_Collection_Programs, Binary_Path_Stores, Finite_Presented_Collections | Keys and tables as structure: a bit is a shape (the empty payload or a pair of empty payloads), a path is the data list of its bits (`path_term`, `path_term_injective`), and a path store is the existing binary store presented as structure (`store_term`: the empty store a leaf, a node its optional value beside its two children). The native search of a store along a key, in a context (`native_store_search_rules`: found at the empty path, left on the one bit, right on the other), with its contract for every key (`native_store_search_program.exact`: a search holds exactly when the key is a path at which the store holds a value checked in the context), and the path store of a list of rows (`path_store`; `path_store_found`: a value found is a row's; `path_store_lookup`: exact on single-valued rows). Executable paths and stores with their readers (`finite_path`, `finite_path_bits`, `finite_store`, `decode_finite_store`), their injectivity and formation (`finite_store_option_injective`, `finite_store_injective`, `finite_path_formed`, `finite_store_option_formed`, `finite_store_formed`). Every path store is canonical (`path_store_canonical`), so two single-valued listings of one set of rows build the same store whatever their order and repetitions (`path_store_rows`). The one row and table presentation: a row as its path with its value (`finite_store_row`) and a table as the store of its listing (`finite_listing_store`), with injectivity, formation and decoding; stores with equal presentations agree at every path up to the value presentation (`finite_store_lookup_agree`), and over single-valued listings whose values are presented injectively on the values present (`inj_on`), a table's presentation is exact on the rows it holds (`finite_listing_store_identifies`, `finite_listing_store_exact`): a table's word is a function of its rows, not of the listing chosen. The readiness table and the development tables are its instances. Reuse (Keys and tables as structure — 2026-09-19): A bit is a shape and a path is the data list of its bits (`path_term`, injective, formed); the binary path store is presented as structure (`store_term`), and its native search along a key in a context is a family of three ordinary rules (`native_store_search_rules`) whose local contract is proved once for every key term (`native_store_search_program.sound`, `.exact`: the search holds exactly when the key is a path at which the store holds a value checked in the context). Any native definition that looks a value up by a key instantiates it; the keyed walk stays for tables whose keys are not paths. A store's presentation reads only the values it holds (`store_term_cong`), and a row presenter is injective on the rows whose values it presents injectively (`finite_store_row_injective_on`, of which `finite_store_row_injective` is the case of every value). |
| Native_Path_Store_Indexes | Carrier_Indexes, Native_Path_Stores | The path store is the native carrier of the index notion. Host part: `path_store_carrier_index` is `carrier_index` with rows over paths, formation `single_valued (set rows)`, queries `UNIV`, key `id`, index `path_store`, search `store_lookup … = Some v`, discharged by `path_store_lookup`. Native part, in the context of `native_store_search_program` (not a sublocale, since it is conditional on the values' formation): `index` is `native_carrier_index` of that host part with site `(k,Pair_Term x (Pair_Term t j))∈positive_meaning P`, context formation `term_formed`, keys presented by `path_term` (distinguishing by `path_term_injective`), stores by `store_term val`, checker the program's `ch` at `val v`, under `⋀y. term_formed (val y)` (from `exact`); `index_sound` is `index_search_sound` of the same, for every `val` (from `sound`). Reuse: an interpretation `X` of `native_store_search_program` made in a theory importing this one inherits `X.index` and `X.index_sound`, as `readiness_settled_searches` and `reach_searches` do (their theories import this one since task 74); then `query_search`, `site_query` and the notion's other laws at the program. |
| Factor_Finite_Development_Questions | Factor_Development_Criterion_Sources, Finite_Binary_Values, Native_Collection_Programs | A question has a subject and a scope: the scope is a native program holding of any subject paired with each candidate (`finite_scope_rule`: its premise is the guard's universal recognizer, so the scope never restates the subject; `finite_scope_program_meaning`, `finite_development_source_meaning`), and a question of a subject judges each candidate by its conditions on the subject (`finite_subject_question`, `finite_subject_question_conditions`). The finite original value and facet families of the earlier questions are the question of the empty subject; actual generation determines scope coverage. A candidate's index is the binary presentation of its natural (`finite_development_index`), one payload whose size grows with the number of digits. Reuse (A native question evaluates what its requests demand — 2026-09-19): The unary natural data term it replaced grew the source quadratically and made the scope review's input ten times larger; the question reads only the equality of indices (`finite_development_index_eq`), which the presentation's injectivity supplies. Reuse (The selection question evaluates native readiness — 2026-09-19): The questions over finite value families are the question of the empty subject. |
| Factor_Source_Development_Admission | Factor_Source_Development_Cycle, Factor_Workflow_Evidence_Meaning | Admission preserves every old artifact and binding, the entire target meaning and the ordered complete query answer. |
| Factor_Source_Development_Cases | Factor_Source_Development_Admission, Factor_Source_Development_Input | Native cases cover source installation, duplicate targets and answers, ambiguity and failed prerequisites. |
| Factor_Source_Development_Cycle | Factor_Source_Development_Subjects, Factor_Steered_Development | The computed native producer selects and installs an actual source, then uses it for the original subsequent query. |
| Factor_Source_Development_Input | Factor_Source_Development_Subjects | Complete typed constructors retain every source, target program, clause, material and query field. |
| Factor_Source_Development_Subjects | Factor_Finite_Development_Questions, Factor_Finite_Source_Entry_Installation | Complete original source-change subjects derive all candidate observations and target selection conditions. |

Under the reading's own boundary, that every site with a row lies in the universe, both return the same sites and the same rows there (`finite_demanded_readings_exact`). Whenever the traversal returns it has read a closed set of sites, with no universe (`finite_demanded_readings_closed`); a closed universe containing the roots bounds it (`finite_demanded_readings_within`, `_exact`). A request demands the calls its applications premise wherever they lead (`finite_program_call_closure`: `_requests`, `_closed`, `_term`, `_ready`, `_available_ready`), which programs pairing components into new arguments need. Reuse (A native question evaluates what its requests demand — 2026-09-19): The calls that requested calls demand (`finite_program_demanded_calls`): the third instance of the frontier traversal of `Finite_Demanded_Closures`, after package and proof-graph readings. The requests are demanded (`finite_program_demanded_calls_requests`), and wherever the term demand is ready so are the demanded calls (`finite_program_demanded_calls_ready`), so an evaluation over them answers every request by its positive meaning wherever the term demand's did. Retained: where the term demand is not ready the demanded calls may or may not be, and wherever they are ready they answer every request exactly. |
| Keyed_Demanded_Sites | Keyed_Finite_Sets, Member_Tree_Indexes, Finite_Demanded_Closures | Re-cited: `keyed_fold_insert` folds the member tree's update instance (`member_tree_insert`). The traversal of demanded sites with its visited sites in an ordered member index kept across steps and the successors of its frontier joined as one sorted listing of keys (`keyed_demanded_sites`); it returns exactly the sites of `finite_demanded_sites` for every key with a left inverse (`keyed_demanded_sites_exact`). |
| Factor_Demanded_Package_Readings | Finite_Demanded_Closures, Factor_Recovered_Graph_Sharing, Factor_Formation_Once_Definitions | A native package reads a definition only at the sites its roots reach. The definition grammar supplies the traversal's premise (a site with a definition reading is a position of the environment, `finite_definition_reading_position`), so the frontier traversal returns the original package sites and definition graph (`finite_demanded_definition_readings`), checking environment formation once; these are the code equations of `finite_native_definition_sites` and `finite_native_definition_graph`, replacing the reading of a definition at every position of the environment. Reuse (The package reading is demanded — 2026-09-19): The package instance. Before, a definition was read at every position of the environment, including every address of every literal target, so reading a policy cost the payload it admits. |
| Listed_Set_Unions | Main | A union computed once and then only read is executed as the concatenation of the listings it joins (`listed_union`, equal to the union by definition); the union of the images of a listed set is built in one pass over its listing (`listed_image_union`). The listing may repeat a member present in two parts, so an accumulation iterated to a fixed point keeps the library's union. Reuse (Unions computed once are listed — 2026-09-19): The library executes the union of two listed sets by inserting one listing into the other member by member, each insertion tested against every member present, so a union of many parts costs the square of the whole. `listed_image_union` joins the images of a listed set in one pass (`listed_image_union_code`, from `listed_image_union_fold`). Retained limits: the listing is not canonical and may repeat a member present in two parts, so a consumer that lists or compares the result sorts it again, and an accumulation iterated to a fixed point keeps the library's union. Membership is still a scan of the listing; a consumer asking many membership questions of one set uses the existing ordered member index (`Ordered_Member_Trees`) instead. |
| Factor_Constructed_Program_Applications | Factor_Finite_Program_Applications, Listed_Set_Unions, Finite_Relation_Functionality_Execution | The applications of a formed call are constructed and not verified again. The shape of a pattern (`finite_pattern_fits`: its pairs and literal leaves) decides its instance under its own match, so a formed term is accepted exactly when it fits and the rows the match returns are functional (`finite_pattern_accepts_fits`, over `finite_matching_rows` and `finite_matching_functional`, through `relation_rows_functional`); a clause whose head covers its variables has at a formed call exactly the instance its shape gives (`finite_constructed_instance`), and the construction checks that coverage itself (`finite_requested_constructed`); an instance is admitted exactly when the interfaces accept the call and every premise call (`finite_admitted_constructed`, `finite_interface_fits`), because every value bound is a subterm of the call and every premise call an instance of formed patterns with those values. Together, the constructed requests of a clause are its admitted ones (`finite_constructed_requests_exact`) and, for a formed program and a formed call, the constructed applications of a call are its applications (`finite_constructed_applications_exact`). What is replaced is `finite_schema_call_formed`, whose `finite_pattern_accepts` carries `finite_term_formed x`: at a formed system and a formed call the formation of a constructed value and of a constructed premise call is never established again, and what remains of the acceptance is each pattern's shape and the equalities at a variable's repeated occurrences, one traversal per premise per clause. The applications of a demand are listed call by call without comparing them (`finite_program_applications_listed`, a code equation over `listed_image_union`), and premise functionality is the functionality of a relation (`finite_premise_functional_rows`). Reuse (A formed call's applications are constructed, not verified again — 2026-09-20): `finite_constructed_applications` returns the applications of one call and `finite_constructed_requests` those of one clause there; `finite_interface_fits` asks an interface's acceptance of a formed term without reading the term's values again; `finite_pattern_fits` and `finite_matching_functional` are that acceptance split into the pattern's shape and the equalities at a variable's repeated occurrences. Retained limits: `finite_program_applications` and `finite_premise_functional` keep their original meanings on their whole declared domains — the installed equations are unconditional and total, carrying their formation tests in their own guards, and the construction returns no application for an unformed call, an unformed system or an uncovered head (`finite_program_applications_unformed`, `finite_program_applications_unformed_system`). The listed union is not canonical and may repeat a member, so a consumer that lists or compares the result sorts it again. |
| RRA_Listed_Environment_Positions | Listed_Set_Unions, RRA_Finite_Environment_Positions | The positions of an environment are the listed union of the positions of its artifacts (`finite_environment_positions_listed`, the abstract code of the finite set), so each carrier is listed once instead of being inserted address by address into the positions already collected. Reuse (Unions computed once are listed — 2026-09-19): Environment positions are read by the fresh-coordinate, readiness, anchor and judgment readers of certified causes; with a literal target of thousands of addresses in the environment they had cost the square of that target (application readiness 0.049, 0.189 and 0.764 seconds at payloads of 1,601, 3,201 and 6,401 addresses; 0.002, 0.003 and 0.007 listed). |
| RRA_Inserted_Attachments | RRA_Finite_Syntax_Construction | Attaching a record or family structure to an artifact inserts the structure's members into the artifact (`finite_attach_structure_inserting_code`, the same union with its operands exchanged), so a wrapper costs the artifact's size for each member it adds instead of the square of the artifact. Reuse (A question's comparison and wrapper are asked through indexes and insertion — 2026-09-19): The library executes a union by inserting each member of its left operand into its right one; attaching a structure to an artifact put the whole artifact on the left, so each record or family wrapper cost the square of the artifact. The overnight review's enumerated union (`Native_Control_Syntax_Candidate`) is an exact alternative that sorts both operands; exchanging the operands needs no order and no listing, and the join of syntax forests, measured cheap, keeps the library's union. |
| Factor_Indexed_Readings | Factor_Formation_Once_Definitions, Factor_Demanded_Package_Readings, Development_Answer_0ccf746fe2cf, Binary_Store_Indexes, Member_Tree_Indexes | Re-cited: the incidence, bindings and counted values are one instance of the index notion, `address_rows_index` (address-headed rows, the relation store read through `address_binary_path`, with `address_rows_member`), giving `reading_heads_indexed`, `reading_values_indexed`, `reading_counted_indexed`; the carrier is the member tree's instance. Every syntax reading asks an artifact the same questions at an address (the incidence rows headed there, the functional values attached there, whether a counted value is attached there, carrier membership) and bounds its recursion by the carrier's size; the reading of an artifact (`artifact_reading`) collects the answers. The scanned reading asks them by traversing the artifact, the indexed reading answers them through the address-keyed relation stores and the ordered member index built once, and the two are equal (`indexed_artifact_reading_exact`); the walk answer's indexes are the indexed reading's fields (`indexed_data_walk_reading_code`). The syntax bodies are stated once over a reading (`read_*`, each the formed-once body under the scanned reading, `*_scanned`); the readings of a use take the readings of the artifacts there (`artifact_readings_at`) and union over them as the formed-once readings union over the artifacts, so they are those readings for every environment (`read_*_at`), and the formed-once readings take them once where a reading begins (the `*_formed_read_code` equations). The guarded entries keep their code equations. Reuse (An artifact is asked through its reading — 2026-09-19): Every syntax reading of an artifact asks it the same questions at an address: the incidence rows headed there, the functional values attached there, whether a counted value is attached there and whether the address is in the carrier, and pattern and term readings bound their recursion by the carrier's size. The scanned reading asks by traversing the artifact; the indexed reading answers through the indexes the walk answer introduced (the address-keyed relation stores of `Binary_Relation_Stores` under `address_binary_path` and the ordered member index of `Ordered_Member_Trees`), built once per reading, and the two are equal (`indexed_artifact_reading_exact`, from the walk answer's `indexed_heads_exact`, `indexed_values_exact`, `indexed_counted_exact`; it restates from the instance `address_rows_index` what those state, since the answer's theory is not edited). Found by attribution: reading an installed ground program back asked 40 to 80 whole-artifact scans per clause (85% of the time at 128 rows); at 16, 32, 64 and 128 payload rows the package read now takes 0.006, 0.019, 0.070 and 0.327 seconds, against 0.031, 0.287 and 3.766 seconds at 16 to 64 rows before. A first version replaced the guarded entries' code equations; the repository check found every word of the seed recipe changed, because the seeded state reads the code equations in effect of its roots and seven of its ten roots are those entries, so that version would have answered seven seeded refinement problems outside the loop. Refining the readings the entries guard, which the seeded state keeps on its frontier, is the move the walk answer made for its own subject. Retained limits: the readings are built per reading of a use, so a package whose definitions share one artifact builds them once per definition site; environment formation is still checked by comparing every artifact row with every other (0.104 seconds at 3,117 addresses), and a package read still traverses its demanded sites three times (sites, formation, graph); a reading's fields are functions, so a reading has no presentation and is never a subject of a report. |

Investigation basis, repairs and retention compute each profile once, use ordered row indexes and ask the comparison relation through its ordered index; the repairs order their rows against each candidate pair joined with each distinct observation pair once (`investigation_loss_order`, `investigation_select_loss_order`), which selects the same rows as the list of every pair joined with every observation row. Every list result is equal. Reuse (A question's comparison and wrapper are asked through indexes and insertion — 2026-09-19): The basis, repairs and retention ask the comparison relation through its index. The repairs order their rows against every candidate pair joined with each distinct observation pair once (`investigation_loss_order`): selecting from a list reads only the last occurrence of each member, and the last occurrences of the two lists have one order (`investigation_loss_order_remdups`, from `remdups_concat_remdups` and the library's `remdups_append2` and `remdups_map_remdups`), so the same rows are selected (`investigation_select_loss_order`) from a list of the pairs times the distinct observation pairs instead of the pairs times every observation row. |

| Ordered_Term_Comparison | Ordered_Finite_Terms, Ordered_Artifact_Comparison | Executable terms compare structurally and stop at the first differing subterm, exactly in the order of their prefix keys. |
| Right_Ordered_Terms | Ordered_Term_Comparison | Terms are ordered at the right component of a pair first, as the order of their mirrored terms, which exchange the components of every pair; the mirror is injective, so the order is linear, and it is computed on the term itself (`finite_term_compare_right`). Keys of calls ordered so tell two calls of one context apart where they differ. Their equality is decided by the same comparison. Reuse (Calls keyed where they differ; the reach of a state — 2026-09-19): A linear order on executable terms comparing a pair's right component first: the order of the mirrored term (`mirror_term`, an involution), computed on the term itself (`finite_term_compare_right`, `finite_term_compare_right_mirror`), with the equality of ordered terms decided by the same comparison. Keys over terms whose left components are shared use it. It rests on the convention of the programs building the terms (a notion passes its context on the left), not on a property of terms, so it is chosen per key and never as the equality of all terms: decided globally, it made the comparison of presented generations, which share their right components, 15 percent slower. |

## Export content boundaries

Content that export theories previously held now lives in a base theory without the
shared refinement import, so refinement changes rebuild only thin export theories.

| Isabelle_Constant_Closure | Main | The shared traversal of a checked Isabelle context: the kernel definitions of a constant, the code equations in effect for it, and the closure of constants reached through the items each contributes. Exporters choose the items; an answer frame notes the code equations in effect as its fact through the same reading, which takes the declared equations as every function transformer receives them, untransformed and unrewritten; the traversal establishes no semantic claim. Reuse (Answers framed where they are adopted; declared code equations — 2026-09-18): The code equations in effect for a constant, read exactly as declared, moved here from the exporter so the exporter and every answer frame read them in one way. Under the global context the certificate had been rewritten by every simplification rule; an answer's own equation could be read as the incumbent. Certified alone (empty simpset, no function transformers) the reading silently returned nothing for Suc-pattern equations under Code_Target_Nat; the code graph's certificate is not local (callees' sort demands). The declared equations are now taken from the list `Code.get_cert` hands every function transformer, untransformed, unoverloaded as certification does. |
| Isabelle_Terms | Finite_Presented_Coordinates | Checked Pure types and terms as structural subjects, with abstractions keeping no bound name. A term is stated over what presents its types (`'ty isabelle_term_with`; the kernel's term `isabelle_term` carries a type at every occurrence), so a term over positions of a table of types is the same datatype, read back by its own map. Name, sort, type and term presentations compose the generic payload, sequence and pair presentations and are injective members of their notions’ classes. Reuse (A state holds each type once — 2026-09-19): A term over positions of a table of types is the same datatype, and reading it back is the datatype's own map, so no second term notion or translation is introduced. |
| Isabelle_Entities | Isabelle_Terms, Finite_Inference_Development | Entities of a checked context: base and development constant declarations, kernel definitions, non-definitional specifications and code equations in effect. Constants, equation heads and subjects are read structurally; reachability from roots is the least closure of ordinary finite inference rules, and undeclared, malformed and unreached entities are computed observations. A rooted state is presented as its ordered roots with its context, once, for every report that presents one. A declaration's term (the constant with its declared type) is read separately from the constant it declares. An entity is stated over its term (`'t isabelle_entity_with`), as a term is over its types. Reuse (Answers framed where they are adopted; declared code equations — 2026-09-18): One presentation of a rooted state (ordered roots and context), reused by the loop presentation and the syntax judgments instead of composing it inline. |
| Isabelle_Readers | Isabelle_Entities, Finite_Presentation_Readers | Exact readers of the presentations of names, positions, sorts, types, terms, entities and contexts (`isabelle_entity_reads`, `isabelle_context_reads`): a presented entity arriving from anywhere is read as the entity it presents or refused as a whole. Constructors are identified by the payload their presentation begins with, read as a value and compared. Reuse (Native answers and their readers — 2026-09-19): Exact readers of names, positions, sorts, types, terms, entities and contexts; a constructor is identified by the payload its presentation begins with, read as a value. |
| Isabelle_Type_Tables | Isabelle_Entities, "HOL-Library.RBT" | A definition holds each distinct type of its terms once: a table of type nodes whose arguments are positions of earlier nodes, read in order into an ordered index (`isabelle_type_table`) and exact at every position of an ordered table (`isabelle_type_table_exact`); a state or terms presented over positions of that table are read back by the entity's and the term's own maps (`isabelle_shared_context`, `isabelle_shared_terms`), so every occurrence of a type reads one value. Reuse (A state holds each type once — 2026-09-19): Which table presents a state is the exporter's transport, as its name table is. |
| Isabelle_Acceptance | Isabelle_Renaming, Factor_Finite_Ground_Evaluation | Acceptance proved once for the notion: the presented entities of a checked context are the ground clauses of one native program whose entry holds exactly of their presentations, so its known calls are exactly the accepted entities; native evaluation decides demanded entities exactly and always answers. The resulting equation (the demanded entities split by membership in the supplied ones) is the notion's code equation, so every use consumes the contract instead of installing and compiling a program again. Reuse (The acceptance decision consumes its contract — 2026-09-18): `isabelle_demand_acceptance_members`: the decision is total and equals the demanded entities split by membership in the supplied ones. It is derived from the notion's existing exact theorem and the ground-source totality, and it is the code equation of the notion, so every execution consumes the contract instead of installing, compiling and reading back a program to decide membership again. |
| Isabelle_Entity_Export | Isabelle_Type_Tables, Isabelle_Constant_Closure | The exporter inside a checked context: it follows the shared closure, translates types and terms constructor by constructor, and defines the entities of a development state, its roots and the roots of each named group as ordinary definitions of that context, all against one name table and each holding every distinct type once in a table of type nodes (the kernel repeats a type at every occurrence). The same roots can be exported again in a later context that extends the defining one; the constants a given theory introduces are then expanded and seeded too, so an answer state holds their specifications, and an introduced constant no root reaches is an unreached entity rather than an invisible one. Reuse (The first real answer and its indexes — 2026-09-18): An answer's introduced constants are the logical constants its theory declares; an abbreviation declares none (pending edit, parked in `.build/impl4/exporter-abbreviations.txt` until the adoption check completes). Reuse (A state holds each type once — 2026-09-19): Every exported state, its roots and its groups are defined through a table holding each distinct type once; a term over any presentation of its types is translated by one function (`term_with`), of which the kernel's form (`term_term`) and the table's form are instances. The context construction takes further terms for its name table, so a caller presenting propositions against the same table (`Native_Control_Syntax_Statements`) reuses it instead of building a second context. Reuse (Pure's constants are base constants — 2026-09-19): A constant belongs to the fixed base when the session of the theory that declared it is a base session (`declaring_session`): a theory's long name is qualified by its session except the theory Pure's, whose long name is its session, so reading the qualifier alone had made every constant of Pure a development constant. |
| Isabelle_Renaming | Isabelle_Acceptance, Inference_Embeddings | A correspondence moves every name of a context to another position while the table holds the same name there. Each reading — positions, constants, equation heads, subjects, declarations, reachability, and the whole assessment and acceptance — is the image of the original reading, so no decision depends on a position or on table order. Reversal is one concrete correspondence; the dependence of an equation reading on the fixed base names is exposed, not hidden. |
| Isabelle_State_Difference | Isabelle_Renaming | Two states read from different checked contexts correspond through the names both tables hold: each position of the first table is embedded at the position of the same name in the second, injectively when the tables have no repeated name, and as a correspondence when the second holds every name of the first. The removed and added entities of the second state account for both states completely, and a state read against any renaming of itself removes and adds nothing. Reuse (Native questions settle through keys; answers are judged by their state difference — 2026-09-18): The correspondence of two checked states through the names both tables hold, and their removed and added entities; the difference accounts for both states completely and is empty against any renaming of a state (`isabelle_state_renamed_unchanged`). The read-state locality of a theory change in the stage-1 gate is an instance of this notion. |
| Isabelle_Local_Names | Isabelle_State_Difference | A value presented with the names it uses: local names and the local embedding through the existing table embedding, invariant under every correspondence of tables (`isabelle_local_embedding_renamed`, `isabelle_local_entities_renamed`); renamings compose and the identity fixes a value; a table extended by the names it lacks (`isabelle_appended_names`) and the embedding back from it (`isabelle_state_embedding_back`); a root presented by the same notion (`isabelle_local_root`, through the one-entity wrapper `isabelle_local_root_entities`, invariant by `isabelle_local_root_renamed`); and the comparison of local presentations across two states whose tables differ, not only by a correspondence: for positions in range and a second table without repeated names, two one-entity presentations are equal exactly when the second entity is the first moved by the table embedding (`isabelle_local_entities_compared`), and likewise for roots (`isabelle_local_root_compared`), with its helpers `isabelle_local_names_in_range`, `isabelle_state_embedding_named` and `isabelle_name_position_member`. The comparison is what the verdict's removed, added and roots fields consume. Stated once for publication loci and payloads, repaired requests, native answers and packets. Reuse (Native answers and their readers — 2026-09-19): The presentation of a value with the names it uses, stated once (moved out of `Development_Publication` at its third use), with the identity and composition of renamings and the table extended by the names it lacks. |
| Factor_Finite_Ground_Evaluation | Factor_Finite_Ground_Source, Factor_Finite_Native_Evaluation | Every clause an installed ground source places at its entry is an alpha variant of a placed exact recognizer, without premise or variable, so native evaluation answers every demand at the entry of any installed ground source; the exact evaluation contract still states the answer. Reuse (The acceptance decision consumes its contract — 2026-09-18): Every clause an installed ground source places at its entry is an alpha variant of a placed exact recognizer, so it has no premise and no variable; native evaluation therefore answers every demand at the entry of any installed ground source. It consumes the installation contract (`finite_source_native_run`), the renamed clause family and `schema_family_variant_origin`; it decides no demand, and the exact evaluation contract still states the answer. |
| Isabelle_Code_Equations | Isabelle_Entities | The two statement readings of an entity that the shared subject reading does not distinguish: the executable-content reading (the proposition of a code equation, refusing definitions, specifications and declarations) and the definition reading (`isabelle_definition_proposition`, the proposition of a kernel definition, moved here from the repair at its second use). Reuse (The seeded refinement contracts and the filtered question — 2026-09-18): The entity language's code-equation reading, separate from the subject reading that a definition of the same constant shares. It refuses every other entity kind and states nothing about the equation it returns. |
| Development_Problems | Isabelle_Entities | A problem is a structural subject of the state — the constants it concerns — with its contract, origin and authority, presented injectively. Dependencies are ordinary finite inference rules whose rows fire only once their own problem is answered, so settlement is the least closure of answered problems with settled prerequisites and readiness is computed from the actual premises; ready problems are pairwise independent. Problems without a subject, problems reaching undeclared constants and residual problems are retained as computed observations. Reuse (The first loop selects, groups and requests natively — 2026-09-18): Settlement is corrected: a dependency row fires only once its own problem is answered. Before, a problem without prerequisites was settled with nothing answered, so a problem depending on it was ready before its prerequisite had an answer; on the seeded state, which has no prerequisites, readiness is unchanged. Ready problems are pairwise independent (`development_ready_independent`). |
| Development_Loci | Native_Path_Stores, Development_Problems, Factor_Finite_Payload_Literals | A locus is a path and nothing else: a role prefix, a kind prefix and the key of the constant the row is about, in that order (`development_locus`). A role is the family that holds a row (`development_role`, five constructors) and a kind is the family that holds it (the five `development_contract` constructors); both are three-bit prefixes stated rather than computed from a numbering (`development_role_path`, `development_kind_path`), of one fixed width, which is what makes the three parts recoverable (`development_locus_parts`, `development_locus_composed`). No program compares a kind or a role, and the kind prefix reads a contract's constructor and nothing inside its term (`development_kind_path_cases`, `development_kind_path_inert`). Each part is determined by the locus (`development_locus_role_determines`, `_kind_determines`, `_key_determines`), and the key assignment is a parameter of which only injectivity on the constants in question is asked (`development_locus_injective`), so nothing here waits on the state that assigns it. A problem, its issue, its request and its answer stand at one locus under four role prefixes (`development_locus_shared_tail`). The locus of a problem is stated exactly where its subject is one constant and nowhere else (`development_subject_constant`, `development_problem_locus_at`, with `_exact`, `_absent` and `development_without_subject_has_no_locus`), the partiality the state already retains. The presented locus is `path_term` of its bits, whose only octet anywhere is the empty payload, read by the octet audit's own `Factor_Finite_Payload_Literals.finite_term_payloads` on its executable presentation `finite_path` (`development_locus_payload_octets`), formed and injective (`development_locus_term_formed`, `development_locus_term_injective`). Reuse (The development notions are structure; a kind is a family and an identity is a path — 2026-09-20): The first of the entry's four restated contracts, which every later one instantiates: rows are keyed by a locus, the request search descends it, the presenters carry it. A family realized as a fixed-width prefix of a path is the reusable shape — a variable-width prefix would let a longer role and a shorter kind spell the same path as a shorter role and a longer kind — and injectivity over a supplied key assignment under `inj_on` is the shape `Development_Native_Selection.development_readiness_key_injective` already uses, so no definition of this line fixes the assignment. |
| Development_Row_Presentations | Development_Rows | The executable presenters of development rows, composed from the generic pair and sequence presentations and the path store's `finite_path`, `finite_store_option` and `finite_store`, each stating injectivity and nothing else: a citation (`development_citation_data`, an absence the store's own optional value) and a family of citations (`development_citations_data`); the bodies of a problem (`development_problem_body_data`, origin and authority citations beside the contract term under a supplied inert presentation), a request (`development_request_body_data`, support and context) and an issue (`development_issue_body_data`, the applied decompositions); a row as its locus with its body (`development_row_data`) and a table as the path store of its rows (`development_table_data`), abbreviations of the store's own `Native_Path_Stores.finite_store_row` and `finite_listing_store`, whose injectivity, formation and decoding they consume (a table is exact on the rows it held, `finite_listing_store_exact`). The problem body is injective on the contract terms present (`development_problem_body_data_injective`, `inj_on` as the relation's `development_rows_inert_injective` asks). Formation of every presenter, and decode lemmas equating each body with the relation's (`development_problem_body`, `development_request_body`, `readiness_decompositions`). No tag, no name-table position, nothing the locus says |
| Development_Rows | Development_Loci, Development_Publication | The presentation relation for development rows (`development_rows_present`), in the shape of `readiness_presents`: the constant key, the state's row key and the inert presentation of a contract term are parameters, each asked only `inj_on` over the values presented; the store's single-valuedness and the formedness of every row value are premises (`development_rows_formed`), owned by whoever presents a state; the distinctness of problem loci is a premise (`inj_on (development_located_at key Development_Problem_Role) (set ps)`); every condition is over actual rows of the path store. Three bodies, grouped as `development_problems_present`, `_requests_present`, `_issues_present`: a problem's body is its origin citation, its authority citation and its contract term carried inert (`development_problem_body`), its subject and kind being the locus; a request's is its support and its context, two families of citations (`development_request_body`); an issue's the family of premise-locus families, read in readiness's own `readiness_decompositions`. A citation is a path, an absence the store's own optional value (`development_row_citation`, over `store_option_term`), a family the data list of paths (`development_row_family`), all injective. Residuals and generated problems are shapes of the stored row (`development_rows_residual_shape`, `_generated_shape`), replacing `development_residual_problems`' comparison by the test that the origin cited is the empty payload. A locus under any role determines its problem (`development_rows_same_locus`); the store determines requests (`development_rows_request_recovery`) and issues up to their premise sockets (`_issue_recovery`), and across two presentations the subject, contract, citations, residual status and authority of a problem (`development_rows_problem_recovery`). Readiness is consumed at the locus: any injective change of keys carries a `readiness_presents` to another (`readiness_presents_rekey`), so `development_readiness_presents` and `native_development_ready` hold at the locus key unrestated (`development_rows_readiness_presents`, `development_rows_native_ready`). Reuse (The development notions are structure; a kind is a family and an identity is a path — 2026-09-20): the second of the entry's four restated contracts, without the answer's body: an answer's added rows have an identity only in the answer state, and its reading as removed and added rows is the reduction of an edit, stated where the comparison across two states is; the answer family follows that; the decomposition's schema and the verdict's contract range over it, and `readiness_presents_rekey` is the shape for moving any readiness presentation to another key assignment. |
| Development_Located_Rows | Development_Rows, Factor_Native_Equality, Factor_System_Relocation | The request at a locus is one search, not a name lookup. The development's store is the path store of its rows, each value presented as itself (`development_rows_term`, over `store_term id`), read by one program (`development_rows_program`, `finite_rule_program` of `development_row_definitions`, a `finite_rule_program_family` instance at each site, `development_rows_family`) with `Native_Path_Stores`' search at `development_row_search` and a check at `development_row_check`, two sites readiness does not use; no program selects a family, compares a role or a kind, or reads a name, the role and the kind being prefixes of the path it descends. The check is the native equality program relocated: its one clause (`native_value_rule`) is an alpha variant of `native_equality_schema`, and `development_row_check_exact` (`(check,Pair_Term x y)` holds exactly when `x` is formed and `x=y`) follows from `native_equality_exact` through `system_variant_renamed_meaning_at` and `positive_meaning_shared_definitions`. `development_row_lookup_at`: on formed rows the search at a locus holds exactly of the value the store's lookup returns there, from `native_store_search_program.exact` and `store_term_cong`; `development_row_at` is it composed with `path_store_lookup` for a single-valued store. `development_request_at`: under `development_rows_present`, the search at a problem's request locus holds exactly of that problem's request body and of no other value — it consumes `Development_Rows.development_rows_request`'s lookup, so "the single request of that subject" is the lookup's functionality. `development_request_problem_locus`: a problem and its request stand at one locus under two role prefixes, from `development_rows_problem`, `development_locus_parts` and `development_locus_shared_tail`. Reuse: every row of the store, of whichever role, is read by the same search at its locus; a use asking the issue's or the answer's value instantiates `development_row_at` with the row the presentation places there. |
| Development_Native_Readiness | Native_Path_Store_Indexes | Readiness as a native definition, a closed finite rule program over rows: a row holds a key that is a path of shapes, a status that is structure (an answered problem's a leaf, an open problem's a pair of leaves) and the decompositions as lists of premise paths; a table is the path store of its rows (`readiness_table_term`). Settlement (an answered row of the table one of whose decompositions has every premise settled) and readiness (an open row all of whose decompositions have every premise settled in the table it is judged with) are composed of the native collection notions at their own sites (every and some element in a context, the search of a path store) and three rules of its own; a row is found by descending the store along its key, and the program states no octet but the empty payload. Contracts for every single-valued table: `native_settled_exact` (the least closure `table_settled`), `native_every_settled`, `native_ready_exact`. The table is the path store's index (`readiness_table_lookup` is its `query_search`), and the settled search's step is `site_query` of the inherited native instance `readiness_settled_searches.index`. Its finite presentation (`finite_readiness_row`, `finite_readiness_table`, instances of the store's `finite_store_row` and `finite_listing_store` with the readiness row value) and its condition of its own (`native_readiness_condition`, `native_readiness_condition_exact`). | RRA_Finite_Transactions | RRA_Replacement, RRA_Finite_Generation_Projections, Finite_Singleton_Selection | The executable finite instance of selection snapshots and structural transactions: finite snapshots of finite generations, their loci, formation (at most one generation per locus), lookup through the singleton reading, the four transaction fields, comparison, update, the observed comparison and the executed transaction, each proved to be the original operation on the decoded values (`finite_transact_exact`); replacement, admission and the transaction that expects what was read at one locus. Reuse (Publication is a transaction against the published state — 2026-09-18): The executable instance of `RRA_Selection` and `RRA_Transaction` over finite generations: snapshot formation (at most one generation per locus, `fcard` of the loci), lookup through the existing singleton reading, comparison, update and observed comparison, each equal to the original on the decoded cores, so `finite_transact_exact` gives every structural transaction theorem to the executed one. `finite_locus_transaction` is the transaction a publisher builds from what it read at one locus: replacement of that generation, or admission when the locus was absent. | RRA_Formed_Snapshot_Transactions | RRA_Finite_Transactions | A formed snapshot stays formed through its successful transactions (`finite_transact_applied_formed`), so a transaction on it checks only itself (`finite_transact_formed`, `finite_transact_formed_exact`); publishing several generations in turn (`finite_locus_publications`) checks the formation of the snapshot it starts from once (`finite_locus_publications_code`, stated for the snapshot alone, so a publisher made once for a snapshot checks it once for every list it publishes) instead of at every step. Reuse (A problem is decomposed through the constants its answer needs — 2026-09-21): The loop's one computed decomposition read as a library row from the history's repair records, with no change of the loop or its presentation; the definition problems keep their empty prerequisite rows, through which alone an answered problem settles. | Development_Publication | Development_Successor, Isabelle_Local_Names, RRA_Finite_Transactions, Factor_Finite_Data_Syntax, Finite_Presented_Structures | The contract of a problem presented with its names (`Isabelle_Local_Names`), invariant under every correspondence of tables (`development_local_contract_renamed`); the locus of a problem is its contract with its names; development generations are finite generations whose targets are complete data quotations (`development_data_target`); a generation is published at its problem's locus by the transaction expecting the incumbent it was judged against, which replaces exactly that locus (`development_publication_applied`) or yields the complete observed comparison when the locus has moved (`development_publication_conflict`); a generation whose locus the published state does not hold is admitted by the transaction that expects that absence (`development_publication_admitted`); decisions stand at loci of their own kind, the issue locus of a problem and the development's selection locus, proved distinct from every problem locus (`development_decision_loci_distinct`), and distinct formed loci are distinct targets because a complete data quotation determines its value (`development_data_target_injective`); presentations of generations, snapshots and transaction results. Reuse (Publication is a transaction against the published state — 2026-09-18): The names a presented value uses, carried with it: the existing table embedding into the list of the names the value mentions; its local contract is invariance under every correspondence of tables (`development_local_contract_renamed`, `isabelle_local_entities_renamed`), with renaming composition and congruence on the positions a value uses as the reusable steps. A problem's locus is its contract with its names, so every problem posed about one constant stands at one locus. Development generations are library generations whose targets are complete data quotations; incumbents are base generations; an admitted answer is published by the transaction expecting the incumbent it was judged against, which replaces exactly its locus or yields the complete observed comparison (`development_publication_applied`, `development_publication_conflict`, both instantiated from `RRA_Replacement` through the exactness theorem, not proved again). Causes are recorded judgments (acceptance, verdict); their validity under the policy is a separate judgment. | Development_Certified_Generations | Development_Publication, Development_Policy, Factor_Certificate_Policy_Readiness, Factor_Finite_Native_Proof_Construction | Recording any presented payload under the policy that lists it is one construction (`development_payload_generation_with`, contract `development_payload_generation_certified`), which families, incumbents, answers and decisions instantiate. A policy's judgment of a payload (`development_policy_judgment`): a certificate constructed from the policy's program for the demand of its definitions on the payload (`development_policy_certificate`; the demand of the entry alone is not closed), replayed, its scope quoted and checked against the policy. The judgment is a function of the listed presentations and the payload alone, and recording it in an environment of generations beside cited predecessors (`development_recorded_generation`) is the existing certificate-to-policy composition (`development_certified_generation_route`), so the recorded cause is a certified call of the policy (`development_certified_generation_certified`) and the payload one of the listed presentations (`development_certified_generation_listed`). A family's judgment is keyed by its presentation with its names (`development_family_key`, `development_payload_judgment`), constructed only for entities of the checked context (`development_family_generation_certified`); incumbents are base generations in environments of their own (`development_certified_incumbent_base`) and accepted answers are recorded beside the incumbent they were judged against and cite the issue of their request, which cites that incumbent (`development_answer_citations`, `development_certified_answer_accepted`), each stated through a supplied judge equal to the judgment. Recording is stated once over the record constructor (`development_payload_generation_using`, `development_answer_using`); in a formed environment whose cited rows read back, the library's known-predecessor constructor records the same generation (`development_payload_generation_using_known`, `development_answer_using_known`, instances of `finite_construct_known_original_generation_exact`), and recording leaves a formed environment that includes the one recorded in and reads the recorded generation back (`development_payload_generation_recorded`, `development_incumbent_with_recorded`, `development_rows_carried`). Reuse (Development causes are certified — 2026-09-19): A policy's judgment of a payload is separated from recording it. The judgment (`development_policy_judgment`) constructs a certificate from the policy's program for the demand of its definitions on the payload (the policy's entry calls the listing entry on the same payload, so the demand of the entry alone is not closed), replays it, quotes the replayed scope and checks it against the policy with the known-scope condition of `Factor_Known_Replay_Policy`; it depends on the listed presentations and the payload alone. `development_certified_generation_route` proves judgment-then-recording to be the library's `certificate_policy_record` composition, so every contract of that composition holds without being proved again. The earlier draft restated that composition by hand and checked the recorded cause by reading it back: 13.7 of about 20 seconds per generation at a seed payload. A family's judgment is keyed by the family's presentation with its names, and incumbents and answers are stated through a supplied judge equal to the judgment, so a use judges each presented family once however many generations record it. Retained limits: a family's policy lists only that family, so the recorded cause carries the policy but not the checked context, and the family's acceptance is the constructor's contract (owner question Q1); the cause quotes the replayed scope, which holds the payload twice, once as the policy's literal and once as the call's argument; and an absent result does not distinguish a refused payload from an unavailable input. Reuse (The loop's decisions are admitted generations — 2026-09-19): `development_judged_generation_fields` moved here from the publication, where it had been proved for the one use. Recording is stated over the record constructor (`development_payload_generation_using`), as the library's replay and policy attempts are (`policy_record_replay_from_source`, `digit_history_source_attempt_using`), and a chain that records into environments it has itself recorded, citing generations it has just recorded, executes with the known-predecessor constructor: `finite_construct_known_original_generation_exact` instantiated a third time, after the known history control and the digit history. Its premises (a formed environment, cited rows that read back) are the contract of the recording that produced them (`development_payload_generation_recorded`) carried by inclusion (`finite_check_generation_included`). |

| Development_Answer_Parts | Main | The form of an answer's declared parts, read by Isabelle's own outer syntax in the keyword table of the answer's frame before the answer's theory is processed (`Development_Answer_Parts.check`): the definitions hold only definitional commands (`definition`, `fun`, `function`, `primrec`, `termination`), theorem statements with their proofs and document text, and no declaration or statement names an attribute or a target; the proof holds proof commands only; the equation is one string token; no part names a method that runs ML or holds a control or document antiquotation; and no name the definitions introduce already names a constant or a fact of the frame. It decides the form of the parts, never their meaning. Reuse (Answers are confined to their declared parts — 2026-09-19): The declared parts of an answer are read by Isabelle's outer syntax with the keywords of the context the answer is framed in, never by the host: tokens, commands and their kinds are Isabelle's own (`Token.explode`, command tokens, keyword tokens), so the reading follows every command the frame's imports declare. It refuses by form alone: commands outside the declared kinds, attributes and targets in declarations and statements (they would act beyond the answer's own constants once its theory is adopted), a proof that continues past its lemma, an equation that is not one string token, a method that runs ML, and control or document antiquotations. The meaning of the parts stays the verdict's. A name the definitions introduce must not already name a constant or a fact of the frame (`Sign.intern_const`, `Global_Theory.intern_fact`), since the adopted answer would change what it reads as in every importing theory. Retained limits: the packet does not yet state the declared form to the executor, and methods defined by later theories that run ML would have to be added to the refused names. |

| Development_State_Rows | Isabelle_Local_Names, Development_Constant_Verification | Definitions: `entity_kind`, `entity_kind_of`, `state_key`, `state_row`, `state_family`, `state_rows`, `presented_rows`, `entity_declared`, `entity_mentions`, `root_mentions`, `entity_row`, `root_row`, `state_positions`, `keyed_agree`, `atoms_present`, `rows_present`, `roots_present`, `state_presents`, `keys_shared`, `kinds_present` (the root's local presentation `isabelle_local_root` is `Isabelle_Local_Names`'s). Contracts: `state_presents_row`, `state_presents_row_origin`, `state_presents_citations`, `state_presents_root_row`, `state_presents_cited_atoms`, `state_presents_root_cited_atoms` (from the inclusions `entity_declared_positions`, `isabelle_entity_subjects_positions`, `entity_mentions_positions`, `root_mentions_positions`), `state_presents_distinct_names`, `state_presents_distinct_roots`, `state_presents_unknown_positions`, `state_presents_families_disjoint`, `state_presents_kind_family`, `state_presents_kind_only`, `kinds_present_rows`, `keys_shared_atom`, `keys_shared_row`, `keys_shared_entity`, `keys_shared_embedding`, `entity_row_renamed`, `state_presents_renamed`. A checked state as the relational skeleton the verdict of a kind reads and no more: constants as atoms keyed by a path and carrying their names inert, entity rows in one family per kind of the entity language and one family for the roots, and `declared`, `subjects` and `mentions` as citations of those atoms with the statement inert. A kind is the family that holds a row, never a datum inside it, so the verdict's two kind arguments are selections of families (`kinds_present`). `state_presents` carries every condition as a premise and computes none; three consequences are derived here, each with the owner of its premise named: the verdict's ninth field (`state_presents_distinct_names`), whose premise the answer's reader owns, as it must refuse a duplicated name; distinct roots (`state_presents_distinct_roots`), whose premise the roots' exporter owns; and the vacuity of the assessment's unknown positions (`state_presents_unknown_positions`), owned by the presentation itself. `keyed_agree` is one notion for keys equal exactly when their values are, instantiated at the atoms by name and at the rows by identity; `keys_shared` puts two presented states over one set of atoms, and `state_presents_renamed` carries a presentation across a table correspondence by consuming `isabelle_local_entities_renamed` rather than proving that invariance again. |
| Development_State_Presenter | Development_State_Rows, Development_Entity_Keys | `state_presents` realized as a partial function, keyed once. Definitions: `state_constant_key` (the binary digits of a constant's table position), `state_presentable` (the three carried conditions: distinct names, owned by the answer's reader or the exporter; every used position in the table, owned by the state's source; distinct local roots, owned by the roots' exporter; executable by `state_presentable_code`), `state_rows_of`, `state_presenter`. Contracts: `state_presenter_presents` (returned rows present the state under `state_constant_key`), `state_presenter_defined`, `state_presents_presentable`, `state_presenter_exact` and `state_presenter_exact_any_key` (rows exactly when the state has a presentation at all), `state_presenter_entity_key` (every entity's row stands in its kind's family at `development_entity_key (snd S) e`, the key the contract question and the requests' context citations use), `state_presenter_root_key`, `state_local_entities_injective` (one state's local presentations identify its entities, `isabelle_local_entities_compared` at equal tables). Entity rows keyed by first occurrence in the entity list, root rows by first occurrence in the root list; no term presentation added (`state_row_term`, `state_family_term` present the rows); a pair of states sharing keys is not stated here. Presents `development_seed_state` and `development_machinery_state` (all three conditions hold, by execution). |
